| 4 | `/dev-team:reference-intake Approved. Next idea: a second stage for the same project, focused on return structure and classical portfolios, no ML. First, PCA decomposition of returns to remove the market and other dominant factors, then Marchenko–Pastur random-matrix denoising of the correlation matrix, and covariance estimation (sample, shrinkage, denoised) compared on real data. Then minimum-variance, risk parity and hierarchical risk parity built on those estimates, compared out of sample on realized variance. The goal is to get variance optimization right before any return prediction.

I've put my reference code for this in references/: market_decomposition.py.` | D320–D350 | U01 |
